In God we live, move, and have our being, 301. See *Jehovah* and *The Lord*. See also the Table of Contents, Part 1.

<column-break/>

**GOOD.** All that proceeds from love is called good, 31. All good belongs to love, 84, 402, 406; or to spiritual heat, 253. Man cannot do good from himself, but only from the Lord, 355. All good proceeds from the Lord, and none from man, 394. All good things which exist in action are called uses, 336. All the power of good is through truth, 406. Good is within truth and acts by means of truth, 406.
**GRANDFATHERS** and **GREAT-GRANDFATHERS.** Inherited tendencies toward evil <note>original: "hereditary evils"</note> are derived from fathers, as well as from grandfathers and great-grandfathers, 269.
**GRATUITOUS GIFTS.** In the heavens, all the necessities of life are given for free, 334.
**GREATEST.** In the greatest and in the least things, the Divine is the same, 77—82. The greatest things in which there are degrees of both kinds <note>discrete and continuous degrees</note>, 225.
**HABITATIONS** of the Lord, 170; with man, 395.
**HABITATIONS** of angels and spirits are determined by how they receive love and wisdom, 121. An angel, unlike a person in the world, knows his house and his dwelling wherever he may go, 134.
**HANDS.** In the Bible <note>original: "the Word"</note>, hands signify power, and the right hand signifies superior power, 220; the right hand corresponds to the "good of truth," and the left to the "truth of good," 384, 409, 127. The "work of the hands of Jehovah" signifies the work of Divine Love and Wisdom, 59.
**HEAD.** The head directs the body according to its will, for the will and understanding reside in the head, 25. Head downwards and feet upwards <note>referring to a state of spiritual inversion or disorder</note>, 275. Three heads on one body, 24.
**HEAR**, to, refers to attention and the act of listening, which belongs to the understanding, 363.
**HEARING** is achieved by means of the lowest atmosphere, which is called air, 176. Hearing occurs in the ear, not in the place where the sound originates, and is a reaction <note>original: "affection"</note> of its substance and form, 41. Hearing does not leave the ear to catch the sound, but the sound enters the ear and affects it, 41. Hearing is not something fleeting <note>original: "volatile"</note> flowing from its organ, but is the organ itself considered in its substance and form, 41. The sense of hearing communicates immediately through nerve fibers with the brain, and from there draws its sensitive and active life, 365. See *Sense*.
**HEART.** The heart and the lungs are the two sources <note>original: "fountains"</note> of the motion of life, 291. As long as the heart beats, love with its vital heat remains and preserves life, 390. The more deeply the heart is examined, the greater are the wonders discovered within it, and the perfections according to discrete degrees, 201. The heart corresponds to the will, 378; and also to love or goodness, 402.

The hells are not distant from man, but around him, and even within those who are wicked, 343. See also 339, 341.

An Infinite without infinite things within Himself is only infinite in name, *ibid*. **Jehovah** is Being <note>original: "Esse"</note> Itself, uncreated and infinite, <insert>4</insert>. **Kingdom,** Mineral, Vegetable, and Animal, 61, 65, 313, 314, 316.
**Kingdoms,** the two kingdoms of the Lord in the heavens: the celestial and spiritual, <insert>101, 232, 381</insert>. The celestial kingdom is called the heart-related <note>original: "cardiac"</note> kingdom of the heavens, and the spiritual the lung-related <note>original: "pulmonic"</note> kingdom, 381. To these two kingdoms is added a third in which men exist, which is called the natural kingdom, 232.

It is with every man by creation and thus by birth; and united to rationality, it distinguishes him from beasts, 240, 264. It belongs not to man, but to the Lord in him, <insert>116</insert>, 425. **LOVE,** to love is to feel another’s pleasure as our own; but to feel our own delight in another, rather than their delight in ourselves, is not love, 47.
**LOVE** is life itself, 1–3, 399, 406. It is the very being <note>original: "esse"</note> of human life, 14, 358, 368. The essence of all love consists in union <note>original: "conjunction"</note>, 47. The union of love arises from reciprocation, 48. Love consists in wanting what is one's own to belong to another and feeling that person’s delight as if it were one’s own, 47. Love has "use" <note>use: a central concept in Swedenborg's work, meaning purposeful service or function</note> as its end and intention, and it accomplishes use through wisdom, 297. Love without wisdom is like an essence without a manifestation <note>original: "esse without an existere"</note>, 139. Love and wisdom are a real and actual substance and form, and they constitute the subject itself, 40, 224. Celestial love is love toward the Lord, or the love of what is good, 426, 427. Those who possess this love have wisdom written into their very lives, 427, 428. Love and wisdom are not abstract ideas; they do not exist outside of a subject, but are rather the states of that subject, 209, 224. Love toward the Lord consists solely in applying the commandments of the Word <note>the Word: the Holy Scripture</note> to one’s life, the sum of which is to flee from evils as sins, 237. By this love is meant the love of performing useful services, 426. *See also* 141, 142, 427. Spiritual love is love toward the neighbor, or the love of truth, 426, 427. Those who possess this love have intelligence written into their lives, 427, 428.

<column-break/>

Love toward the neighbor is the spiritual love of performing uses, 237. By that love is meant the love of uses, 426. Natural love, as opposed to spiritual love, is the love of self and of the world, 424, 416. Natural love, when separated from spiritual love, becomes sensual and focused only on the body, 424. The love of self and the love of the world are hellish <note>original: "infernal"</note> loves, 396; however, they are heavenly by creation because they are the loves of the natural man, which are meant to serve spiritual loves just as foundations serve houses, 396. In each kingdom of nature, the lowest things exist for the use of the middle things, and the middle things for the use of the highest, 65.

The spirit of a man is truly a man because it is capable of receiving love and wisdom from the Lord, 287. Spiritual man, natural man, and spiritual-natural man, 250–255. The spiritual man is entirely distinct from the natural man; there is no communication between them other than that which exists between a cause and its effect, 251. Thus, there is a natural, spiritual, and celestial mind, 239, 260. The natural mind is composed of both spiritual and natural substances, 257, 260, 270, 273. It surrounds and contains the spiritual and celestial mind, 260. Its primary principles reside in the brain, 273. It moves the body and all its parts at will, 387. In its form or image, it is a world; the spiritual mind, in its form or image, is a heaven, 270. The spiritual mind derives its form only from the substances of the spiritual world, 270. The natural mind turns in spiral coils from right to left, but the spiritual mind turns from left to right, 270. See the *Contents, Parts 2 and 5.*

Effort, power, and motion are joined according to discrete degrees, and this connection is not by a smooth transition <note>original: "continuity"</note>, but by correspondences, 218. See *Effort and Power.* Heart <note>original: "cardiac"</note> and lung movement, 381.

**NATION.** Each nation in the spiritual world has its place based on its concept of God as a man, 13.

**NATURAL.** Everything that exists and remains from the sun of the natural world is called "natural," 159. Nothing natural exists that does not come from a spiritual cause, 134. The natural man, 251. The spiritual-natural man, 429. The sensual-natural man, 144, 162, 254. How the natural man becomes spiritual, 248.

**NATURALISM.** Where it comes from, 69.

**NATURE**, in itself, is completely inactive <note>original: "inert"</note>, 166. It is completely dead, 159, 340. If it appears to be alive in humans and animals, it is because of the life that accompanies and moves it, 159. Everything in nature proceeds from love and wisdom, 46. Nature contributes nothing at all to the creation of plants and animals, 344. It has not produced, and cannot produce anything; rather, the Divine has produced and produces all things from Himself.

**PERPETUITY**, the, of every divine work arises from the union of love and wisdom, **36**.

**FANTASY** <note>original: "Phantasy"; in Swedenborg’s usage, this refers to delusive or hallucinatory appearances that seem real to the person experiencing them</note>, what it is, 424.

**PLEURA**. Its relation to the heart and lungs, 384, 402, 403.

**PLURALITY OF GODS**, impossible, **27**.

**POISONS**. From where they have originated, 339.

**POSTERIOR THINGS**. See *Prior Things*.

**POWER** is effort <note>original: "endeavour"; referring to the Latin *conatus*, a striving force</note> put into action; it is produced by effort and produces motion, 218. Living powers in man are what constitute the internal parts of his body, 219. It is contrary to order for a dead power to act on a living power, **166**. Perfection of powers, **200**. Active, intermediate, and passive powers, **178**. See 311, 340, 344, 392.

**PREACHER**, **148**.

**PRESENCE** of the Lord, how it is everywhere, 299. Presence of angels, the way in which it takes place, 291. Man can, by means of thought, be present as if he were elsewhere; it does not matter how remote the place is, 285.

**PRIMARY PRINCIPLES** <note>original: "Primaries"</note>, the, of life are in the brain, 365. These primary principles are the will and the understanding, 365. All things and every single thing of the animal kingdom are primary principles, **65**. See *Mediates and Ultimates*.

**INITIAL FORM** <note>original: "Primitive"; refers to the biological and spiritual starting point of a human life</note>, the, of a man is the seed of the father, by which conception is achieved, 432. What it is in the uterus after conception, 432.

**DERIVATIVES** <note>original: "Principiates"; from the Latin *principiata*, meaning things that are derived from or organized by the primary "principles" or origins</note>. All things of the body are derivatives; that is, they are compositions of fibers from "principles" (origins), which are receptacles of love and wisdom, 369. The will and understanding are, in their derivatives, in the body, 362, 365, 387, 403. Wherever the principles lead, the derivatives follow; they cannot be separated, 369.

**PRINCIPLES** <note>original: "Principles"; here meaning the primary origins or sources of life</note>, the, of the life of man are the receptacles of love and wisdom, 369. By life, in its principles, is meant the will and the understanding, 365. The will and understanding are, in their principles, in the brain, 362, 365, 387, 403. Just as life is in its principles, so it is in every single part, 366. Life, through its principles, is from every part in the whole, and from the whole in every part, 367.

**PRIOR THINGS** are composed of their first elements, **208**. They are more perfect than posterior things, 204. From prior things we may view posterior things, but not the reverse, **119**.

**PROCEEDING**. The first thing proceeding from the love and wisdom of the Lord is spiritual fire, which appears before the angels as a sun, **97**, **152**, 290, 300. See *Divine Proceeding*.

**PRODUCTION**, the, of seeds was the first production from the soils <note>original: "earths"</note>, 312.

**PROGRESSION** of everything in the universe from its primary principles toward its ulti-

<column-break/>

mates <note>the most outward or physical forms</note>, and from its ultimates toward its primary principles, 304, 314, 316.

**PROPAGATION** of subjects of the vegetable and animal kingdom, 347.

**PROPOSITIONS**, abstract, being universal, are usually better understood than those which are applied to specific examples, 228.

**SELFHOOD** <note>original: "Proprium"; a central term in Swedenborg meaning the "own-self," or the ego that feels independent of God</note>, the, of man and of the angels is evil, **114**. This selfhood, which is self-love, opposes the influx and reception of the divine; it hardens the heart and closes it, 335.

**PROVINCES**, the, of heaven are distinguished according to the members, internal organs <note>original: "viscera"</note>, and organs of man, 288.

**PULMONARY PIPES** <note>referring to the trachea and bronchial tubes</note>. Their existence in microscopic insects, 373.

**PULSE**, 378. The spirit of man has a pulse as well as the body, and this pulse flows into the pulse of the body and produces it, 390, 391. There is a correspondence between them, 390.

**PURIFICATION** of the love in the understanding, how it is achieved, 419, 420. All purification of man is achieved by the truths of wisdom, 420. Purification of the blood, 420, 423.

**QUALITY**. That which has no form has no quality, and that which has no quality is not anything, **15**, 223.

The different reception of love and wisdom decides the quarter in the spiritual world, **126**. Man, as to his spirit, is in a certain quarter of the spiritual world regardless of which quarter of the natural world he may be in, **126**.

**RAMIFICATIONS** of the bronchi <note>original: "bronchia"</note> of the lungs, 405, 408, 412. They correspond to the perceptions and thoughts proceeding from the affections of truth, 405.

**RATIONALITY** is the faculty by which man can understand what is true and what is good; it is the faculty of the understanding, 240, 264, 413, 425. It is with each man by creation, and thus by birth, and when united with liberty, it distinguishes him from the beasts, 240, 264, 413. It is with the wicked as well as with the good, 266. It is never taken away from man, 247, 258. It does not exist in a man before his natural mind has reached maturity, 266. Nor with the man...

**TOUCH**, the sense of, is not in the objects that are applied to it, but is in the substance and form of the skin, which are the subjects that experience it, **41**. This sense is solely the state <note>original: "affection"</note> of the subject produced by the things which have been applied to it, **41**. The sense of touch communicates immediately by fibers with the brain, and draws from it its sensitive and active life, **365**. See *Sense*. To touch with the hand signifies to communicate, **220**.

**TRACHEA** <note>The windpipe</note>, **382**, **408**.

**TRANSMISSION** of the love of evil from parents to their children, **269**.

**TREES and SHRUBS.** How they are produced, **346**. There are in them degrees of both kinds <note>Both discrete degrees of distinct levels and continuous degrees of gradual change</note>, **225**. Streams of invisible particles <note>original: "effluvia"</note> flow unceasingly from them, **293**.

**TRINE.** In everything of which anything can be stated <note>original: "predicated"</note> there is a trine <note>A three-fold structure</note>, which is called end, cause, and effect, **209**, **154**, **167—172**, **296—301**.

**TRINITY**, the, in the Lord, is called the Father, Son, and Holy Spirit; the Divine itself is called the Father; the Divine Human, the Son; and the Divine Proceeding, the Holy Spirit, **146**.

**TRUTH.** All that proceeds from wisdom is called truth, **31**. Truth is nothing other than the form of the emotion <note>original: "affection"; in this context, the outward expression of an inner desire</note> which belongs to love, **411**. Truth belongs to the understanding, **406**, **410**. All truths belong to spiritual light, **253**.

**TRUTHS**, apparent, are appearances according to which everyone may think and speak; but when they are accepted as real truths, they become falsehoods and illusions, **108**.

**TURN**, to. The angels continually turn their faces toward the Lord, **129—134**. All their internal parts <note>original: "interiors"</note>, both of the mind and body, are turned toward the Lord as a sun, **135—139**. Every spirit, whatever his quality, turns himself toward his ruling love, **140—145**.

**TYRE**, in the Word <note>The Bible</note>, signifies the Church in terms of its knowledge of good and truth, **325**.

**ULTIMATE** <note>The final or lowest level of a series, which acts as the foundation for those above it</note>, the, of each series—which is use, action, work, and exercise—is the completion and container <note>original: "continent"</note> of all the prior principles, **215**. Every ultimate consists of previous things, and these consist of their first principles, **208**. Every ultimate is enclosed in a covering, and is thereby distinct from the things that came before it, **278**. In every ultimate there are discrete degrees in simultaneous order, **207**, **208**. Degrees of height in their ultimates are in their fullness and power, **217—221**. The spiritual ultimate, when separated from its higher principles, produces evil uses, **345**. All things in the mineral kingdom are ultimates, **65**. See *Primaries and Mediates*.

<column-break/>

**UNDERSTANDING**, the, is the receptacle of wisdom, **360**; and of intelligence, **430**. It is an organic form, or a form organized from the purest substances, **373**. It is the light by which love sees, **406**, **96**. It may be in spiritual light even though the will may not be in spiritual heat, **244**. It does not lead the will, but only teaches it and shows it the way, **244**. It does not join itself to the will, but the will joins itself to the understanding, **410**. It corresponds to the lungs, **382—384**. See *Will and Thought*.

**UNION** in One, where it comes from, **15**. Union of love with wisdom, and of wisdom with love, **35—37**; of spiritual heat with spiritual light, and the reverse <note>original: "vice versa"</note>, **99**. Reciprocal union makes unity, **35**.

**UNITY.** Reciprocal union makes unity, **35**.

**UNIVERSAL**, the, of all things is love and wisdom, **28**.

**UNIVERSE**, the, in general has been divided into two worlds: one spiritual and the other natural, **163**. The universe, when considered in terms of uses, is the image of God, **64**, **169**. All things in the universe are recipients of the Divine Love and Wisdom of the God-Man <note>Swedenborg’s term for the Lord as the Divine Source of all human qualities</note>, **55**. Everything that exists in the universe has a correspondence with everything in man, **52**. See the *Contents, Part 4*.

**URETERS** <note>The tubes that carry urine from the kidneys to the bladder</note>. Why there are two, **384**. The one on the right corresponds to the "good of truth," and the one on the left to the "truth of good," **384**.

**USES.** Those things are called "uses" which, proceeding from the Lord, exist in the order of creation, **298**, **307**, **316**, **335**, **336**. All uses, which are the purposes <note>original: "ends"</note> of creation, exist in forms, **307**. The use is like the soul, and the form of use is like the body, **310**. Use corresponds to "good," and its form corresponds to "truth," **409**. All uses are produced by the Lord through ultimates <note>The final, physical results</note>, **310**. Every use in the created universe corresponds to the uses of man, **298**. Evil uses were not created by the Lord, but are produced from hell, **336—348**. All evil uses are in hell, and all good uses are in heaven, **339**. All good things that exist in action are named "good uses," and all evils that exist in action are named "evil uses," **336**. How a person may know whether the uses they perform are spiritual or merely natural, **426**. To perform uses is to act with sincerity, uprightness, and fidelity in the work belonging to the office or job we hold, **431**. See **65—68**; and the *Contents, Part 4*.

**UTERUS.** Formation of man in the uterus, **6**, **365**, **400**. State of the infant in the uterus, **407**, **410**. The uterus is to the child what the earth is to the plant seed, **316**.

**VACUUM**, a, is nothing, **373**, **299**. Conversation of angels with Newton concerning a vacuum, **82**.

It also touches upon the correspondences of various organs and the theological structure of the Trinity.</summary>

<language>English</language>
<page-type>index</page-type>

<page-num>193</page-num>
<header>ALPHABETICAL AND ANALYTICAL INDEX.</header>

**VAPORS.** The action they produce on the blood, **420**.
**VARIETY.** From where the variety of all things in the created universe proceeds, **300, 155**. Variety of things in general, and also of things in particular, **155**. The varieties of love are infinite <note>original: "indefinite"</note>, **368**. Varieties present an image of the infinite and eternal, **318**. Variety obscures <note>This likely refers to variety without an organizing principle or unity</note>, **228**.
**VEGETABLE KINGDOM.** Forms of uses in that kingdom, **314**. The relationship to man in every single thing of the vegetable kingdom, **61**.
**VEGETABLES.** From where they originated and how they are produced, **340, 346, 351**. They contain degrees of both kinds <note>Referring to discrete degrees (levels of existence) and continuous degrees (gradations like light to shade)</note>, **225**. The wonders presented by their production, **60, 61, 340**. Streams of invisible particles <note>original: "effluvia"</note> flow constantly from vegetables, **293**. The vegetables that appear in the spiritual world are pure correspondences <note>In Swedenborg’s thought, "correspondences" are natural things that perfectly represent and reflect spiritual realities</note>, **339**.
**VEGETATION.** How it is produced, **314**.
**VEINS**, **399, 400, 408, 420**. Vena Cava, **405, 412, 413, 415**. Bronchial veins, **405, 407, 413**. Pulmonary veins, **405, 407, 412, 413, 420**. Veins correspond to the affections, and in the lungs, they correspond to the affections for truth, **412, 420**.
**VENTRICLES** of the heart; why there are two, **384, 409**. The ventricle on the right corresponds to the "good of truth," and the one on the left to the "truth of good," **384**. Right ventricle, **405**. Left ventricle, **401, 405, 420**.
**VERTEBRAE**, **408**.
**VESSELS** of the heart, **207, 399, 400, 412**. The blood vessels of the heart in the lungs correspond to the affections for truth, **405**. The air vessels of the lungs correspond to perceptions, **412**.
**VISCERA** <note>The internal organs of the body</note>, **207, 370, 373, 376, 377, 384, 385, 400, 401, 408, 410**. Their composition, **190**. The more deeply the viscera are examined, the greater the wonders discovered within them, including perfections according to discrete degrees, **201**.
**VISIBLE THINGS** in the created universe testify that nature has not and cannot produce anything, but that the Divine has produced and continues to produce everything from Himself through the spiritual world, **349—357**.
**VIVIFICATION** <note>The act of giving life</note>. Why it is said to be accomplished by the spirit of Jehovah, **100**.
**WATERS** are intermediate powers, **178**. In the spiritual world there are waters just as in the natural world, but they are spiritual, **173—178**.
**WAYS** in the spiritual world, **145**.
**WEEKS**, in the Word <note>The Bible</note>, signify states of being, **73**.
**WEST**, in the Word, signifies love toward the Lord when it is decreasing, **121**. In the spiritual world, those who are in a lower degree of love live in the west, **21**.
**WHOLE**, the, exists from its parts, and the parts depend on <note>original: "subsist from"</note> the whole, **367**.

<column-break/>

**WILL**, the, is the container <note>original: "receptacle"</note> for love, **360**. It is the entire man, even in regard to his form, **403**. The will and understanding are distinct from each other, just as love and wisdom are, **361**. They are actual substance and form, not abstract concepts; they do not exist outside of their subjects (which are substances), but are the states of those subjects, **209, 42**. They are organic forms, or forms organized from the purest substances, **373**. They are created so that they may be two distinct things, yet still act as one in every operation and sensation, **395—397**. The will leads the understanding and makes it act as one with it, **244**. The will corresponds to the heart, **378**. See the *Contents, Part 5*.
**WIND.** Why man believes that the soul or spirit is a wind, or something airy like the breath of the lungs, **383**.
**WINTER**, in the Word, signifies the end of the Church <note>The "end of the Church" refers to a period when spiritual life and true faith have declined to their lowest point</note>, **73**.
**WISDOM** is the outward manifestation <note>original: "existere"</note> of life, derived from its essential being <note>original: "esse"</note>, **14, 358, 368**. It is nothing but the form of love, for love makes itself seen and known through wisdom, **358**. It proceeds from love and is its form, **368**. It is the cause of which love is the end, and use is the effect, **241**. It does not produce love, but teaches how a person ought to live and the way in which he ought to walk, **244**. Wisdom without love is like manifestation without an essential being; it is like the light of winter, **139**. It is the role of wisdom to do good from an affection for what is good, **428**. See the *Contents, Part 5*. See also *Love*.
**WISE.** He is called wise in the Word who performs works, **220**. Man will be judged not by the wisdom of his speech, but by his life, **418**.
**WOLVES.** From where they originated, **339**.
**WONDERS.** Through the wonders he sees in nature, every person may confirm his belief in the Divine if he chooses, **351—356**. Wonders presented by the instincts of animals, **60**.
**WORD**, the <note>The Bible</note>. Why the Lord is called the Word, **221**. In the Word there are three levels of meaning <note>original: "senses"</note> corresponding to the three degrees: the celestial, spiritual, and natural, **221**.
**WORD.** In every single word of a language, there is sound, articulation, and meaning, **280**. Every expression in the Word contains a spiritual meaning, which belongs to Divine Wisdom, and a celestial meaning, which belongs to Divine Love, **280**.
**WORK.** In every Divine work there is a union of love and wisdom, **36**.
**WORKS.** Everything belonging to the three degrees of the natural mind is included in "works," **277—281**. From a man's works, we judge the intention <note>original: "thought"</note> of his will, **215**. The entirety of charity and faith is found in works, **214, 215, 220**. This is why works are so frequently commanded in the Word, **215, 220**.

It emphasizes that all physical reality, from vegetables to the human heart, corresponds to spiritual principles of Love and Wisdom.</summary>

<header># ALPHABETICAL AND ANALYTICAL INDEX.</header>

**WORLD.** There are two worlds, the spiritual and the natural, **83, 163**. While they are similar in their external appearance, they are entirely different in their internal nature, **163, 173**. They are so distinct that they have nothing in common; they communicate only by correspondences <note>In Swedenborg's philosophy, "correspondence" refers to the relationship between a spiritual cause and its natural effect</note>, **83**. In the spiritual world, everything exists that is found in the three kingdoms of the natural world, **52, 321**. All these things are correspondences and exist according to the affections of angels and spirits, and according to the thoughts derived from them, **322**. The spiritual world is located where man is, and is not at all distant from him, **92**. Every person, regarding the inner parts of their mind, is in the spiritual world in the midst of spirits and angels, **92**. By the "spiritual world," heaven and hell are understood, **339**; as well as the world of spirits, **140**.

<column-break/>

**WORLD OF SPIRITS,** the, is in the middle between heaven and hell, **140**. Every person after death enters this world, which forms a part of the spiritual world, **140**.
**WORMS,** harmful <note>original: "noxious"</note>, the origin of where they come from, **341, 342, 339**. The marvelous transformation of worms, **354**. Silk-worms, **61, 356**.
**WRITE,** to. There are those who can think and speak well, and yet cannot write well; the reason why, **361**.
**WRITING.** There is nothing in spiritual writing similar to natural writing, except for the letters, each of which contains a complete meaning <note>original: "sense"</note>, **295**. These two types of writing communicate only by correspondence, **806** <note>The number 806 appears in the original, though the main text of this work only contains 432 sections; this may be a typographical error in the 1859 edition or a reference to another work</note>.
**ZENITH.** Why the sun never appears at the zenith <note>The point directly overhead</note> in the spiritual world, **105**.

# Swedenborg’s Theological Works.

## THE SWEDENBORG SOCIETY.

### 36 BLOOMSBURY STREET, OXFORD STREET, LONDON, W.C.

#### ENGLISH EDITIONS.

->*Arranged in the order in which the original works were written and published by the author.*<-

1749-56. **Heavenly Secrets <note>original: "Arcana Cœlestia"</note>: the Heavenly Mysteries**
contained in the Holy Scripture, or Word of the Lord, unfolded in an
exposition of Genesis and Exodus; together with an account <note>original: "relation"</note> of wonderful
things seen in the world of spirits and in the heaven of angels. 12 volumes.
Demy octavo <note>A standard book size, approximately 5.5 by 8.75 inches</note>, £2 8s.

->Any volume separately, 4s.<-

**Index to the Heavenly Secrets <note>original: "Arcana Cœlestia"</note>.** Volume I, 8s. Volume II
is in preparation.

1758. **Heaven and Hell;** also, the **Intermediate State,**
or World of Spirits. An account <note>original: "relation"</note> of things heard and seen. Demy octavo, 3s.
With **Hartley’s Preface,** 3s. 6d.

1758. **The Last Judgment and the Destruction of Baby-**
**lon;** showing that all the predictions in the Revelation are at this day fulfilled;
being an account <note>original: "relation"</note> of things heard and seen. With a continuation regarding
the Last Judgment and the spiritual world. Demy octavo, 8d.

1758. **On the White Horse,** mentioned in the Revelation,
chapter 19, with particulars regarding the Word and its spiritual sense.
Extracted from the *Heavenly Secrets* <note>original: "Arcana Cœlestia"</note>. Demy octavo, 4d.

1758. **On the Earths in our Solar System,** and on the
Earths in the Starry Heavens: with an account of their inhabitants, and also
of the spirits and angels there; from what has been heard and seen.
Demy octavo, 8d.

1758. **On the New Jerusalem and its Heavenly Doctrine,**
according to what has been heard from heaven; to which is prefixed information 
regarding the New Heaven and the New Earth. Demy octavo, 1s.

1763. **Angelic Wisdom concerning the Divine Love and**
**the Divine Wisdom.** Demy octavo, 2s. A new edition, with an extensive index.

1764. **Angelic Wisdom concerning the Divine Providence.**
Demy octavo, 3s. A new edition, with an extensive index.

# SWEDENBORG’S THEOLOGICAL WORKS.

1763. **The Four Leading Doctrines of the New Church,**
represented by the New Jerusalem in the Book of Revelation; being those regarding the
Lord; the Holy Scripture; Faith; and Life. With an introductory preface,
and an account of the author, and the treatise on the *White Horse*. Demy
octavo <note>original: "Demy 8vo"; a standard book size, approximately 5.5 by 8.75 inches</note>, 3s.; foolscap <note>original: "foolscap"; a smaller book size, approximately 4.25 by 6.75 inches</note>, 2s.

Each of the above doctrines may be purchased separately, namely:

1763. **The Doctrine of the New Jerusalem regarding the**
Lord. To which are added answers to questions on the Trinity, proposed by
the late Reverend T. Hartley, Master of Arts. Demy octavo, 1s.

**The Doctrine of the New Jerusalem regarding the**
Holy Scripture. Demy octavo, 1s.

**The Doctrine of the New Jerusalem regarding**
Faith. Demy octavo, 4d.

**The Doctrine of Life for the New Jerusalem, from**
the Commandments of the Decalogue <note>The Ten Commandments</note>. Demy octavo, 6d.

1766. **The Apocalypse Revealed; in which the mysteries <note>original: "arcana"</note>**
foretold there are disclosed. Two volumes, 8s.

1768. **Marriage Love <note>original: "Conjugial Love"; a term used by Swedenborg to describe the spiritual and holy nature of love between a husband and wife</note> and its Chaste Delights;** *also,*
Adulterous Love and its Insane Pleasures. Demy octavo, 4s. A new edition,
with an extensive index.

1769. **A Brief Exposition of the Doctrine of the New**
Church, represented by the New Jerusalem in the Book of Revelation. Octavo, 10d.

1769. **The Interaction <note>original: "Intercourse"</note> between the Soul and the Body.**
Demy octavo, 4d.; foolscap, 2d.

1771. **The True Christian Religion;** *or,* the Universal
Theology of the New Church; foretold by the Lord in Daniel 7:13, 14; and
in the Revelation <note>original: "Apocalypse"</note> 21:1, 2. With indexes of words, names, subjects, and
passages of Scripture; and an index to the notable accounts <note>original: "Memorable Relations"; Swedenborg's descriptions of his experiences in the spiritual world</note>. Demy
octavo, 7s.

->**POSTHUMOUS WORKS.**<-

**The Final Appendix <note>original: "Coronis"; a Latin term for a crowning finishing touch</note> to the work entitled The True**
Christian Religion. Demy octavo, 8d.

**The Apocalypse Explained according to the Spiritual Sense.**
Six volumes, demy octavo, £1 4s. Any volume separately, 4s.

**A Summary Exposition of the Internal Sense of the Pro-**
phetic Books of the Word of the Old Testament and also of the Psalms of David.
Demy octavo, 1s.

**On the Divine Love and the Divine Wisdom.** (Extracted
from *The Apocalypse Explained*.) Demy octavo, 1s.

**On the Athanasian Creed <note>An early Christian statement of belief focusing on the Trinity and the nature of Christ</note>, and subjects connected with it.**
(Extracted from *The Apocalypse Explained*.) Demy octavo, stitched, 1s. 6d.; cloth, 2s.

---

Any work in the above list will be sent postage or carriage free upon receipt of its cost.
Postage stamps may be sent for sums under 5s. Money orders should be made pay-
able to **WILLIAM WHITE, at the Post Office, Bloomsbury, London, W.C.**
